# Runtime Data Ledger rev 3 - group ui-lane

Part of the [runtime data ledger, rev 3](../RUNTIME-DATA-LEDGER.md) (the index: vocabularies, totals, entity model, kernel features, decisions). **Tree:** `D:/wt/ui`, `feat/ui-advanced` @ `615cda8f`. **Rows:** 196, of which **196 active**; 203 non_rows (kept in the group JSON ledger).

Active forms: system-scratch 89, out-of-scope:compile-time 48, diagnostics 17, component 16, resource-column 11, kernel-internal 5, relation 4, out-of-scope:test-only 3, event 2, out-of-scope:os-owned 1. Active classes: R 8, F 40, B 78, D 17, T 2, C 51. Crates: boyko_render 24, boyko_shaderdsl 48, boyko_ui 124. Rungs: OOS 52, R2 5, R4 44, R6 95.

## Lane notes and decisions

- **scope:** crates/boyko_ui/src (45 files); merge-base with the ledger tree ca582e72 is 5ec1699f; rev 3 (item 1): plus every non-test file the lane adds and the added hunks of every non-test file it changes outside crates/boyko_ui (git diff 5ec1699f..615cda8f), with crates/boyko_render/src/ui censused whole because the lane rewrote it
- **census:** regex = the ui-input recount v2 pattern (recount_ui_v2/census.py) + thread_local!, Box::leak, stable sorts, fs::/env::var/PathBuf/OsString/CString, str case/replace, OnceLock/LazyLock/Mutex/RwLock, thread spawn, FixedBitSet/IndexMap; lexer = critic/lex.py (comments and string contents blanked, #[cfg(test)]/#[test] items brace-matched). 45 files, 229 hit lines (code 157, doc 30, comment-or-string 13, test 29); zero-hit files: anchor.rs, binding/components.rs, binding/mod.rs, interaction/action.rs, interaction/components.rs, interaction/mod.rs, interaction/plugin.rs, lib.rs, reload/mod.rs, text/measure.rs, text/mod.rs, text/shape.rs, units.rs, world/components.rs, world/mod.rs, world/project.rs, world/visibility.rs. thread_local! in non-test code: 0 (and 0 in test code); Box::leak / .leak(): 0; static items in non-test code: 0. Rev 3 (item 1): 24 files outside boyko_ui, 145 hit lines (code 111, doc 23, comment-or-string 11) -> 72 rows (65 new, 7 carried from joltab render) and 73 non_rows; script rev3/item1/ui_ext_census.py.

**Notes**

- The lane adds 2 heap rows to boyko_ui/src and removes none: 122 of the ledger's 122 boyko_ui rows are carried (evidence text identical, lines re-anchored). Its ~2.6k new src lines (animation.rs 997, sprite.rs 498, components.rs +738) put their data in ECS forms already: four dense Tween* channels, the UiVisual sink, the dense UiSpriteCursor, the UiNineSlice / UiSpriteSheet / UiSpriteAnim table components and the POD UiClock resource hold no std heap. Of the lane's own 16 commits (+11250/-37 in crates/boyko_ui), +2657 are src and +8579 are tests (ui_a1_source_census.rs alone is 4157).
- The lane is NOT a superset of the ledger's tree: joltab carries 4 commits after the merge-base 5ec1699f that touch boyko_ui (01a4436e, b49c45af, 93190b7b, 70f3de7d; text/vocab.rs +221 lines, serialize.rs, dispatch.rs, reconcile.rs, tree_view.rs, split.rs, text/mod.rs, lib.rs). Consequences: 11 ledger non_rows (serialize.rs private-helper `out: &mut String` params) do not exist in the lane (listed in lane_absent), and the ledger row dispatch.rs:92 has the post-b49c45af text `{name:?}` where the lane still has `{other:?}` (carried as the same row at dispatch.rs:241). A merge of the lane with ke16 needs a re-census of the merged tree, not a union of the two row sets.
- thread_local! pass: 0 thread_local! in boyko_ui/src (non-test and test alike), 0 Box::leak / .leak(), 0 non-test statics; the only statics are layout.rs:1769-1770 inside #[cfg(test)] (a const-initialised Mutex&lt;()> and an AtomicUsize, no heap). No class-T row arises from TLS in this crate.
- Dead datum: D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/reload/state.rs:126 "pub last_report: UiParseReport," is written (plugin.rs:110, reload/system.rs:191) and read by nothing in any crate's src, tests or examples of the lane workspace. Its bytes are the report rows text/report.rs:27/30; delete the field rather than migrate it.
- Refutation of a lane plan statement: the exclusive ui_tween_reap exists because 'Commands would land the removal at the next apply window' (D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:777 "`Commands` would land the removal at the next apply window, which is fine for"). The executor applies a system's commands before it decrements any ordered successor (D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/schedule/schedule.rs:741 "self.systems[i].system.apply(world);", then D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/schedule/schedule.rs:755 "for &successor in self.conflict_graph.successors[i].iter() {"), so the command channel already delivers at the ordering edge. Basis of decision UL-D7.
- Kernel hazards the lane MEASURED that bear on the dense forms this ledger prescribes: D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:448 "takes a bare id with NO liveness check — MEASURED, it removes a live," (DenseStore::remove); D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:454 "2. **This kernel does not recycle [`EntityId`]**" (load-bearing for the generation-free key of UiTweenScratch); D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:515 "it panics at insert." (#[require] of a dense id); D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/sprite.rs:24 "`Changed<C>` inside `Or<..>` was MEASURED never to fire on this kernel" (dense change detection under Or). Any row the ledger moves to dense-component inherits all four.
- Out of this group's scope but superseding another group: the lane also rewrites boyko_render/src/ui (10 files, +2577/-259: gather.rs +541 new, pack.rs +725, upload.rs +696, resources.rs +483) with new heap sites (for example a `staging: Box<[UiInstance]>`, `node_buf: Vec<UiNode>`, `keys: Vec<(u32, u32)>` and gather roots/arch_ids/stack Vecs). The render group's rows for boyko_render/src/ui/{pack,upload,resources}.rs are therefore stale against the lane; a render-lane census is owed.
- Concurrent edit: D:/claude/BoykoEngine/docs/memory/ALLOCATOR-DESIGN-SPACE.md was rewritten at 05:51:50, after the ledger's 05:28 synthesis, and every line the ledger's UI rows cite moved by +21 (322->343, 340->361, 349->370). This group cites the current lines; the ledger's own citations of that file are stale.
- Census accounting: every one of the 229 lane hit lines is a row or a non_row; 18 entries sit on lines the regex does not hit and were carried from the ledger by verbatim text (report.rs:74/80 `reason.into()` rows from the recount's supplementary pass; 16 non_rows the inventory's broader `collect` word pattern produced).
- [rev 3, item 1] The render-lane census this lane owed is done: crates/boyko_render/src/ui (whole), bindless.rs and benches/ui_pack_sort.rs (added hunks / added file), and the boyko_shaderdsl emit_ui bin and emit hunks are rows of this group. 9 joltab render rows are SUPERSEDED by it (render.md).

**Decisions** (rev-2 status in the index, section Decisions)

- **UL-D1** D rows: diagnostics form (ledger section (i), a vocabulary decision) - **decided:** kernel-internal (boyko_log structured record: code + tagged args). **Performance:** Neutral: every D site is on the load/reload path. Chosen for vocabulary closure and to match 257/322 ledger D rows. **Overturned by:** A per-frame caller of UiParseReport::error/warn (they would become F rows on KF-06).
- **UL-D2** T rows: std-internal form (ledger section (i), a vocabulary decision) - **decided:** resource-column: in-house GetFileAttributesExW on a UTF-16 path encoded once into UiHotReload (inline array, no heap). **Performance:** Removes one heap allocation + free + UTF-8->UTF-16 transcode per hot-reload poll (reload/system.rs:123) and one at boot (plugin.rs:132). **Overturned by:** A counting #[global_allocator] on windows-gnu showing std::fs::metadata no longer allocates (the rows are then deleted).
- **UL-D3** Fonts: resource-column vs kernel Assets&lt;T> (ledger section (i), 'the text/asset owner'); extended to the lane's sprite sheets - **decided:** resource-column on the FontTable / UiSheetTable Resources (KF-01 + KF-04). **Performance:** One indexed load per lookup; Assets&lt;T> adds a generational check, a slot indirection and refcount traffic per lookup that no unload path uses. **Overturned by:** A runtime font or sheet unload/streaming path in src.
- **UL-D4** hover_entered: event vs system-scratch (ledger section (i), decided by KF-24) - **decided:** event; KF-24 is built (system-scratch until it exists). **Performance:** One lane append per hover transition; the alternatives are two structural migrations (marker via Commands) or a per-frame clear pass (enable-state). **Overturned by:** KF-24's publish-at-edge measured costlier than the retained Vec at the focus system's event counts.
- **UL-D5** System-scratch backing: ScratchColumn vs FrameArena (ledger section (i), 'Design rev 2') - **decided:** ScratchColumn (per-system, high-water retained, cleared by set_len). **Performance:** Steady state does no work at all (clear keeps the committed pages); ui_focus_system fills six lanes interleaved in one run and layout fills depth-segmented lanes recursively, which a single bump arena cannot grow at once (the design's own critique). **Overturned by:** The resident commit of the UI scratch columns (one granule per column: 64 KiB before the packing plan, 4 KiB after) measured over a memory budget before the packing plan lands.
- **UL-D6** Scratch id minting route (KF-01; ledger section (i), 'One kernel decision') - **decided:** kernel scratch band keyed by Layout, explicit stagger argument. **Performance:** Off the production id counter; bounded band; stagger explicit where it matters (swept physics cohorts), free where it does not (UI lanes). **Overturned by:** Band exhaustion, or a measured conflict-miss rate a per-type id would have avoided.
- **UL-D7** The lane's tween completion list (new row animation.rs:473): where does a finished channel's removal travel? - **decided:** event on the kernel command channel: ui_visual_tick issues Commands::remove::&lt;Tween*>(entity); UiTweenScratch and ui_tween_reap are deleted. **Performance:** One command record + apply per completion against one push + dense lookup; zero on non-completing frames either way. No kernel feature needed: the executor applies a system's commands before decrementing its ordered successors (schedule.rs:741 before :762). **Overturned by:** A1 gate 6 red on the tick's CommandQueue growth that pre-seeding cannot fix, or the A1 tick bench pricing the apply above push+lookup; fallback system-scratch on KF-01.

| crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:473 `done: Vec<(EntityId, ComponentId)>,` | UiTweenScratch::done | `Vec<(EntityId, ComponentId)>` | F | event | widget | kernel command channel (CommandQueue, event form); fallback ScratchColumn | D:/wt/ui/docs/UI-PLAN-ANIMATION-DECISIONS.md:421 "mut done: ResMut&lt;UiTweenScratch>,   // retained completion list; no per-frame alloc" [CONFLICT: the plan holds the completion list as a std Vec in a Resource; superseded by the owner's 2026-09-10 order. This ledger sends the completion through the kernel command channel and deletes the list] | [field; growth highwater; addr_cached no; rung R4] ECS: [DECIDED 2026-09-11, ui-lane.] A per-frame message from ui_visual_tick (producer) to the removal (consumer): 'this channel finished, remove its dense row'. component skipped: a completion is a transition of a channel, not a durable per-entity datum; dense-component skipped: the channels themselves ARE the dense components (TweenTint/Opacity/Offset/Scale) and this is their removal; enable-state considered: a per-channel 'finished' bit would make the remover scan every live channel (O(channels) against O(completions)) and would still need a structural remove, and KF-16 does not make a dense remove legal inside the tick's iteration; relation skipped: no edge. => event, on the kernel's own deferred-structural-op channel: ui_visual_tick issues Commands::remove::&lt;Tween*>(entity) itself. This needs NO new kernel feature: the executor applies a system's commands in the apply window BEFORE it decrements any ordered successor (D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/schedule/schedule.rs:741 "self.systems[i].system.apply(world);" precedes D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ecs/src/ecs/core/schedule/schedule.rs:762 "self.executor_scratch.pred_remaining[s] -= 1;"), so every system ordered after the tick - A3's ui_transition_apply included - already sees 'no row present' the same frame. That refutes the lane's stated reason for the exclusive reap (D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:780 "reads "no row present ⇒ at rest", and an exclusive system in the same set"), deletes UiTweenScratch and ui_tween_reap, and restores the on_remove path the reap bypasses (D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:792 "narrow and it is stated rather than discovered: this path does NOT fire"). Performance: one command record + one apply per COMPLETION (a tween finishes once) against one Vec push + one dense-registry lookup today; non-completing frames cost nothing either way. Overturned if A1 gate 6 (zero allocation on the steady animating path) reds on the tick's CommandQueue growth and a pre-seeded capacity cannot hold it, or if the A1 tick bench prices the command apply above the push+lookup at the completion frame; the fallback is system-scratch, ScratchColumn&lt;(EntityId, ComponentId)> in UiTweenScratch (KF-01). Class: NEW in the lane (rung A1, e7a16fd9). Retained across frames - D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:434 "A `Resource`-owned buffer reused across frames — the [`UiBarScratch`] shape," - so the steady animating path allocates nothing (A1 gate 6); peaks at one entry per completing channel per frame. Keyed by a generation-free EntityId, which the doc comment rests on a measured kernel fact (D:/wt/ui:crates/boyko_ui/src/animation.rs:454 "2. **This kernel does not recycle [`EntityId`]**"). Readers: ui_visual_tick pushes one (entity, channel) pair per tween channel whose elapsed reached its duration (animation.rs:728/737/750/763); ui_tween_reap (exclusive, ordered immediately after the tick in UiAnimationSet) mem::takes it, removes each channel's dense row through dense_registry_mut().store_existing_mut(id).remove(entity), clears it and puts it back (animation.rs:808-818); UiTweenScratch::pending reads its len |

The comparators are total or the lookup tolerates any hit in a duplicate run, so sort_unstable_by_key is a drop-in and this allocation disappears with no storage form at all. component / dense-component skipped: a load/reload-phase intermediate of the loader system (the parse/diff working set), discarded when the phase ends - its final products ARE components on widget entities (lowering spawns them); enable-state / relation skipped: not a bool, not an entity edge; event skipped: produced and consumed inside one loader run; resource-column skipped: rebuilt from empty each load, not durable.  Class: OUTSIDE both census patterns. slice::sort_by/sort_by_key is std's stable driftsort: after an insertion-sort short-circuit for len &lt;= 20 it computes alloc_len = max(max(len - len/2, min(len, 8_000_000/size_of::&lt;T>())), SMALL_SORT_GENERAL_SCRATCH_LEN) and heap-allocates Vec::with_capacity(alloc_len) whenever that exceeds a 4096-byte stack buffer (installed stable rust-src dated 2026-09-03: library/core/src/slice/sort/stable/mod.rs:77-80 and :111-128). Derived from the std source and the type layout, not measured. UiName is `#[repr(C, align(64))]` and 64 B (components.rs:272), so (UiName, Entity) is 128 B and alloc_len >= 48 means 6144 B > 4096: this sort heap-allocates on EVERY call with more than 20 names. lookup_name resolves to any hit in a duplicate run (lower.rs:157 doc: "tolerated here by resolving to whichever hit the search returns"), so an unstable sort is semantically equivalent.
